#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();

    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The stencil uses double precision and keeps boundary cells fixed. I’ll keep both grids on the GPU, map adjacent threads to adjacent cells, and reuse values along Z in registers. I’ll preserve the original arithmetic order and compare complete result hashes with the CPU version.

+constexpr unsigned tileY = 8;

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.y; tile < tilesYZ; tile += gridDim.y) {
+        const size_t y = (tile % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (tile / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];

+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {

+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 8;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.y; tile < tilesYZ; tile += gridDim.y) {
+        const size_t y = (tile % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (tile / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }
     }

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

struct DeviceGrids {
    Real* first = nullptr;
    Real* second = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;

    ~DeviceGrids() {
        if (first) cudaFree(first);
        if (second) cudaFree(second);
        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
    }
};

__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* first, Real* second, size_t count) {
    for (size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         idx < count; idx += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(idx % 19);
        first[idx] = value;
        // Both buffers retain the same fixed boundary for every iteration.
        second[idx] = value;
    }
}

constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
constexpr unsigned tileY = 8;
constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;

// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x + 1;
    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.y; tile < tilesYZ; tile += gridDim.y) {
        const size_t y = (tile % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
        const size_t z = (tile / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
        Real center = input[idx];
#pragma unroll
        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
                bottom = center;
                center = top;
                idx += plane;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    const size_t maxCells = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
        nx > maxCells / ny || nx * ny > maxCells / nz) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid grid dimensions or iteration count\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    const size_t gridBytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
    DeviceGrids grids;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&grids.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&grids.first), gridBytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&grids.second), gridBytes));
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>((gridSize + 255) / 256, 65535));
    initializeGrid<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, grids.stream>>>(grids.first, grids.second, gridSize);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));

    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;
    const size_t tilesY = hasInterior ? (ny - 2 + tileY - 1) / tileY : 0;
    const size_t tilesYZ = hasInterior ? tilesY * ((nz - 2 + tileZ - 1) / tileZ) : 0;
    const dim3 threads(tileX, tileY);
    const dim3 blocks(hasInterior ? static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + tileX - 1) / tileX) : 1,
                      static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesYZ, 65535)));
    Real* input = grids.first;
    Real* output = grids.second;
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    if (hasInterior) {
        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            stencilIteration<<<blocks, threads, 0, grids.stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, tilesY, tilesYZ);
            std::swap(input, output);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double cellUpdates = hasInterior ? static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
    const double mcups = milliseconds > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / (milliseconds * 1000.0) : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        finalGrid.resize(gridSize);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(finalGrid.data(), input, gridBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    }
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+    Real* first = nullptr;
+    Real* second = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceGrids() {
+        if (first) cudaFree(first);
+        if (second) cudaFree(second);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* first, Real* second, size_t count) {
+    for (size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         idx < count; idx += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(idx % 19);
+        first[idx] = value;
+        // Both buffers retain the same fixed boundary for every iteration.
+        second[idx] = value;

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 8;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.y; tile < tilesYZ; tile += gridDim.y) {
+        const size_t y = (tile % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (tile / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {

+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(sum, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }

The first CUDA version matches the CPU hashes for cubic, rectangular, boundary-only, and zero-iteration cases. Its timed stencil computation is about 40× faster on the RTX 3090 for the larger grids tested. I’m now checking kernel tuning and launch overhead while keeping those exact results.

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // The stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative, normal doubles (or zero).
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,

+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;

fma=ctypes.CDLL('libm.so.6').fma
fma.argtypes=[ctypes.c_double]*3
fma.restype=ctypes.c_double
reciprocal=1.0/7.0

    if i<100000:
        value=rng.random()*126.0
    else:
        bits=(rng.randrange(900,1030)<<52)|rng.getrandbits(52)
        value=struct.unpack('d',struct.pack('Q',bits))[0]
    q=value*reciprocal
    result=fma(fma(-q,7.0,value),reciprocal,q)
    assert result==value/7.0, (value,result,value/7.0)
print('Reciprocal plus FMA correction: 300,000 random bounded/normal values match double division exactly')

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,re,statistics
p=Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp')
source=p.read_text()
variants=[(32,8,4),(32,4,4),(32,4,8),(32,8,8),(64,4,4),(64,4,8),(64,2,4),(128,2,4)]
best=None
try:
    for tx,ty,tz in variants:
        s=source.replace('tileX = 32','tileX = '+str(tx)).replace('tileY = 8','tileY = '+str(ty)).replace('tileZ = 4','tileZ = '+str(tz))
        p.write_text(s)
        subprocess.run(['cmake','--build','/tmp/stencil3d_cuda_build','-j','4'],check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        throughputs=[]
        for n,it in [(128,100),(256,30),(512,10)]:
            out=subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/stencil3d_cuda_build/stencil3d','-x',str(n),'-i',str(it)],text=True)
            throughputs.append(float(re.search(r'Performance: ([0-9.]+)',out).group(1)))
        score=statistics.geometric_mean(throughputs)
        print((tx,ty,tz),'MCellUpdates/s:',throughputs,'score:',round(score),flush=True)
        if best is None or score>best[0]: best=(score,(tx,ty,tz),s)
finally:
    p.write_text(best[2] if best else source)
print('Selected',best[1],flush=True)
subprocess.run(['cmake','--build','/tmp/stencil3d_cuda_build','-j','4'],check=True)

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // The stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative, normal doubles (or zero).
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.

+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {

+        const size_t z = (tile / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)

struct DeviceGrids {
    Real* first = nullptr;
    Real* second = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;

    ~DeviceGrids() {
        if (first) cudaFree(first);
        if (second) cudaFree(second);
        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
    }
};

__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* first, Real* second, size_t count) {
    for (size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         idx < count; idx += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(idx % 19);
        first[idx] = value;
        // Both buffers retain the same fixed boundary for every iteration.
        second[idx] = value;
    }
}

constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;

__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
    // The stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative, normal doubles (or zero).
    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
}

// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
__global__ __launch_bounds__(tileX * tileY)
void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x + 1;
    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.y; tile < tilesYZ; tile += gridDim.y) {
        const size_t y = (tile % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
        const size_t z = (tile / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
        Real center = input[idx];
#pragma unroll
        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
                bottom = center;
                center = top;
                idx += plane;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    const size_t maxCells = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
        nx > maxCells / ny || nx * ny > maxCells / nz) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid grid dimensions or iteration count\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    const size_t gridBytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
    DeviceGrids grids;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&grids.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&grids.first), gridBytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&grids.second), gridBytes));
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>((gridSize + 255) / 256, 65535));
    initializeGrid<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, grids.stream>>>(grids.first, grids.second, gridSize);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));

    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;
    const size_t tilesY = hasInterior ? (ny - 2 + tileY - 1) / tileY : 0;
    const size_t tilesYZ = hasInterior ? tilesY * ((nz - 2 + tileZ - 1) / tileZ) : 0;
    const dim3 threads(tileX, tileY);
    const dim3 blocks(hasInterior ? static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + tileX - 1) / tileX) : 1,
                      static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesYZ, 65535)));
    Real* input = grids.first;
    Real* output = grids.second;
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    if (hasInterior) {
        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            stencilIteration<<<blocks, threads, 0, grids.stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, nz, tilesY, tilesYZ);
            std::swap(input, output);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double cellUpdates = hasInterior ? static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
    const double mcups = milliseconds > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / (milliseconds * 1000.0) : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        finalGrid.resize(gridSize);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(finalGrid.data(), input, gridBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    }
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
+    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
+    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
+    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
+    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
+    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
+        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
+        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
+        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
+        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-        }
-    }
 }

+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);

+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
+    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
+    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
+    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
+    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
+    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
+        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
+        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
+        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
+        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-    }

+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
+    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
+    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
+    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
+    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
+    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
+        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
+        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
+        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
+        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
+    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
+    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
+    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
+    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
+    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
+        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
+        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
+        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
+        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }
     }

    16	void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    17	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
    18	        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
    19	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    20	    }
    21	}

    25	struct DeviceGrids {
    26	    Real* first = nullptr;
    27	    Real* second = nullptr;
    28	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    29	    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    30	    cudaGraphExec_t graphExec = nullptr;
    31	
    32	    ~DeviceGrids() {
    33	        if (graphExec) cudaGraphExecDestroy(graphExec);
    34	        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    35	        if (first) cudaFree(first);
    36	        if (second) cudaFree(second);
    37	        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
    38	    }
    39	};
    40	
    41	__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* first, Real* second, size_t count) {
    42	    for (size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    43	         idx < count; idx += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
    44	        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(idx % 19);
    45	        first[idx] = value;
    46	        // Both buffers retain the same fixed boundary for every iteration.
    47	        second[idx] = value;
    48	    }
    49	}
    50	
    51	constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
    52	constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
    53	constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
    54	constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;
    55	
    56	__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
    57	    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
    58	    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
    59	    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
    60	    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
    61	    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
    62	    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
    63	    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
    64	    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
    65	}
    66	
    67	// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
    68	// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
    69	template <bool FlatTiles>
    70	__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
    71	void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
    72	                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
    73	    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
    74	    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
    75	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    76	    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
    77	    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
    78	    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
    79	    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
    80	    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
    81	    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
    82	        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
    83	        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
    84	        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
    85	        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
    86	        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
    87	        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
    88	        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
    89	        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
    90	        Real center = input[idx];
    91	#pragma unroll
    92	        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
    93	            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
    94	                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
    95	                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
    96	                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
    97	                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
    98	                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
    99	                bottom = center;
   100	                center = top;
   101	                idx += plane;
   102	            }
   103	        }
   104	    }
   105	}
   106	
   107	bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
   108	    // Simple sanity checks
   109	    
   110	    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
   111	    for (const auto& val : grid) {
   112	        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
   113	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   114	            return false;
   115	        }
   116	    }
   117	    
   118	    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
   119	    Real minVal = grid[0];
   120	    Real maxVal = grid[0];
   121	    for (const auto& val : grid) {
   122	        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
   123	        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
   124	    }
   125	    
   126	    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
   127	    
   128	    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
   129	    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
   130	        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   131	        return false;
   132	    }
   133	    
   134	    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
   135	    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
   136	    
   137	    return true;
   138	}
   139	
   140	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   141	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   142	    printf("Options:\n");
   143	    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
   144	    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   145	    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   146	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   147	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   148	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   149	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   150	}
   151	
   152	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   153	    size_t nx = 128;
   154	    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
   155	    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
   156	    int iterations = 10;
   157	    bool validate = false;
   158	    bool printResults = false;
   159	    
   160	    // Parse command line arguments
   161	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   162	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   163	            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   164	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   165	            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
   166	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   167	            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
   168	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   169	            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
   170	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   171	            validate = true;
   172	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   173	            printResults = true;
   174	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   175	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   176	            return 0;
   177	        } else {
   178	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   179	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   180	            return 1;
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	    
   184	    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
   185	    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
   186	    const size_t maxCells = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
   187	    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
   188	        nx > maxCells / ny || nx * ny > maxCells / nz) {
   189	        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid grid dimensions or iteration count\n");
   190	        return 1;
   191	    }
   192	    
   193	    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
   194	    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
   195	    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
   196	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   197	    
   198	    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
   199	    const size_t gridBytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
   200	    DeviceGrids grids;
   201	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&grids.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   202	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&grids.first), gridBytes));
   203	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&grids.second), gridBytes));
   204	    
   205	    // Initialize
   206	    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
   207	    const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>((gridSize + 255) / 256, 65535));
   208	    initializeGrid<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, grids.stream>>>(grids.first, grids.second, gridSize);
   209	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   210	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));
   211	
   212	    const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;
   213	    dim3 threads(tileX, tileY);
   214	    if (hasInterior) {
   215	        // Redistribute threads for thin grids rather than leaving most lanes
   216	        // inactive. Remaining threads process independent groups of Z cells.
   217	        while (threads.x > 1 && threads.x / 2 >= nx - 2) threads.x /= 2;
   218	        threads.y = blockThreads / threads.x;
   219	        while (threads.y > 1 && threads.y / 2 >= ny - 2) threads.y /= 2;
   220	        // CUDA permits at most 64 threads in the block's Z dimension.
   221	        threads.z = std::min(64u, blockThreads / (threads.x * threads.y));
   222	    }
   223	    const size_t tilesY = hasInterior ? (ny - 2 + threads.y - 1) / threads.y : 0;
   224	    const size_t tilesZ = hasInterior ? (nz - 2 + threads.z * tileZ - 1) / (threads.z * tileZ) : 0;
   225	    const size_t tilesYZ = tilesY * tilesZ;
   226	    const bool flatTiles = tilesY > 65535 || tilesZ > 65535;
   227	    const dim3 blocks(hasInterior ? static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + threads.x - 1) / threads.x) : 1,
   228	                      flatTiles ? static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesYZ, 65535))
   229	                                : static_cast<unsigned>(tilesY),
   230	                      flatTiles ? 1 : static_cast<unsigned>(tilesZ));
   231	    Real* input = grids.first;
   232	    Real* output = grids.second;
   233	
   234	    const auto launchIteration = [&](Real* source, Real* destination) {
   235	        if (flatTiles) {
   236	            stencilIteration<true><<<blocks, threads, 0, grids.stream>>>(source, destination, nx, ny, nz, tilesY, tilesYZ);
   237	        } else {
   238	            stencilIteration<false><<<blocks, threads, 0, grids.stream>>>(source, destination, nx, ny, nz, tilesY, tilesYZ);
   239	        }
   240	    };
   241	
   242	    // An even batch always returns to the first buffer, so the same graph can
   243	    // be replayed without changing kernel parameters or transferring data.
   244	    // Limit graph size independently of the requested iteration count.
   245	    const int graphSteps = hasInterior && iterations >= 4 ? std::min(iterations - iterations % 2, 128) : 0;
   246	    if (graphSteps != 0) {
   247	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(grids.stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
   248	        for (int iter = 0; iter < graphSteps; iter += 2) {
   249	            launchIteration(grids.first, grids.second);
   250	            launchIteration(grids.second, grids.first);
   251	        }
   252	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   253	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(grids.stream, &grids.graph));
   254	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&grids.graphExec, grids.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   255	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(grids.graphExec, grids.stream));
   256	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));
   257	    }
   258	    
   259	    // Run stencil iterations
   260	    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
   261	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   262	    
   263	    if (hasInterior) {
   264	        if (graphSteps != 0) {
   265	            for (int batch = 0; batch < iterations / graphSteps; ++batch) {
   266	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(grids.graphExec, grids.stream));
   267	            }
   268	        }
   269	        const int remaining = graphSteps != 0 ? iterations % graphSteps : iterations;
   270	        for (int iter = 0; iter < remaining; ++iter) {
   271	            launchIteration(input, output);
   272	            std::swap(input, output);
   273	        }
   274	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   275	    }
   276	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(grids.stream));
   277	    
   278	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   279	    const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
   280	    
   281	    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
   282	    
   283	    // Calculate performance metrics
   284	    const double cellUpdates = hasInterior ? static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2) * iterations : 0.0;
   285	    const double mcups = milliseconds > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / (milliseconds * 1000.0) : 0.0;
   286	    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   287	    
   288	    // Print results for external validation
   289	    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
   290	    if (printResults || validate) {
   291	        finalGrid.resize(gridSize);
   292	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(finalGrid.data(), input, gridBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   293	    }
   294	    if (printResults) {
   295	        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
   296	    }
   297	    
   298	    // Validation
   299	    if (validate) {
   300	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   301	        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
   302	        
   303	        if (valid) {
   304	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   305	            return 0;
   306	        } else {
   307	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   308	            return 1;
   309	        }
   310	    }
   311	    
   312	    return 0;
   313	}

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling Z window saves two
+// neighbor loads per update, while independent Z tiles expose ample parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {

+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// On regular grids, each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling
+// Z window reuses neighbors in registers, and independent Z tiles expose ample
+// parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
+    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
+    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
+    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
+    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
+    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
+        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
+        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
+        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
+        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }
     }

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 2;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 4;
+constexpr unsigned blockThreads = tileX * tileY;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real value) {
+    // Nonzero stencil sums and quotients are well within the normal range.
+    // A rounded reciprocal followed by an exact FMA residual and correction
+    // gives the same rounded quotient as division by 7, without recomputing
+    // the reciprocal in every cell. Division by this odd integer has no ties.
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 1.0 / 7.0;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(value, reciprocal);
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-quotient, 7.0, value);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// On regular grids, each warp accesses consecutive X coordinates. A rolling
+// Z window reuses neighbors in registers, and independent Z tiles expose ample
+// parallelism.
+template <bool FlatTiles>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(blockThreads)
+void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,
+                      size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, size_t tilesY, size_t tilesYZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    if (x >= nx - 1) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    // Normal grids use direct 3D indexing. Flattening with a grid-stride loop
+    // also supports dimensions exceeding CUDA's Y/Z launch limits.
+    const size_t firstTile = FlatTiles ? blockIdx.y : 0;
+    const size_t tileCount = FlatTiles ? tilesYZ : 1;
+    const size_t tileStride = FlatTiles ? gridDim.y : 1;
+    for (size_t tile = firstTile; tile < tileCount; tile += tileStride) {
+        const size_t yTile = FlatTiles ? tile % tilesY : blockIdx.y;
+        const size_t zTile = FlatTiles ? tile / tilesY : blockIdx.z;
+        const size_t y = yTile * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        if (y >= ny - 1) continue;
+        const size_t z = (zTile * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z) * tileZ + 1;
+        if (z >= nz - 1) continue;
+        size_t idx = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+        Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+        Real center = input[idx];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned step = 0; step < tileZ; ++step) {
+            if (z + step < nz - 1) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Keep the CPU's addition order and correctly rounded division.
+                const Real sum = center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                               + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx] + bottom + top;
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(sum);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
+                idx += plane;
             }
         }
     }
